            function slow {

                local s_pname="$1"

                done

                return 0

            # cleanup: Execute cleanup functions and exit with specified code
          
      331.

            # 
          
      332.

            # Runs all registered cleanup functions in order, then exits.
          
      333. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    333. 
            

            

            # Cleanup functions must have names starting with "cleanup_".
          
      334.

            # Can be used as a signal handler.
          
      335. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    335. 
            

            

            # 
          
      336. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    336. 
            

            

            # WARNING: If using the report function here, do not use

    337. 
            

            

            #          a third argument! If you do you will get an

            #          infinite loop.
          
      339.

            #

            # Usage: cleanup exit_code
          
      341.

            # Args:

            #   $1 - Exit code to use when exiting

            # Returns: Does not return - exits with provided code
          
      344.

            function cleanup {
          
      345. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    345. 
            
              8
            

            

                local c_rc="${1:-0}"
          
      346. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    346. 
            
              8
            

            

                print_error_rule
          
      347. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    347. 
            
              8
            

            

                echo "${STAMP}: exiting cleanly with code ${c_rc}. . ." >&2
          
      348.

      349. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    349.

                for cleanfn in "${cleanup_functions[@]}"; do
          
      351.

                    if [[ "$cleanfn" == cleanup_* ]]; then

      353.

    354.

      356.

      357. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    357. 
            

            

                    else
          
      358. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    358. 
            

            

                        echo "${STAMP}: not calling $cleanfn (invalid name)" >&2
          
      359. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    359. 
            

            

                    fi
          
      360. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    360. 
            

            

                done
          
      361. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    361. 
            
              8
            

            

                echo "${STAMP}: . . . all done with code ${c_rc}" >&2
          
      362. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    362.

      363. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    363. 
            

            

            }

            function load_report {

            # kids: Recursively find all child processes of a PID

            function kids {

                local pid="$1"

    168.

                # Check if /proc is available

                if [[ ! -d "/proc/$pid" ]]; then
          
      173.

      181.

      182. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    182.

    186.

            # apply_niceload: Apply load limiting to a process and its children
          
      194.

            #

    195. 
            

            

            # Uses GNU niceload to limit system load caused by a process tree.
          
      196. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    196. 
            

            

            # Tracks controlled processes in a file to avoid duplicate control.

            # Requires niceload command and optional OPT_NICELOAD global variable.

            #

            # Usage: apply_niceload main_pid workers_file target_load
          
      200.

            # Args:
          
      201. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    201. 
            

            

            #   $1 - Main process ID to control
          
      202. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    202. 
            

            

            #   $2 - File path to store controlled PIDs
          
      203.

            #   $3 - Target system load limit

    204. 
            

            

            # Returns: 0 on success
          
      205. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    205. 
            

            

            function apply_niceload {
          
      206. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    206. 
            

            

                local an_mainid="$1"
          
      207. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    207. 
            

            

                local an_workers="$2"
          
      208. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    208. 
            

            

                local an_target_load="$3"
          
      209. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    209. 
            

            

                
          
      210. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    210. 
            

            

                parallel_not_empty "top level pid for niceload" "${an_mainid}" || return $?
          
      211. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    211. 
            

            

                parallel_not_empty "file to store the pids of the workers" "${an_workers}" || return $?
          
      212. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    212. 
            

            

                parallel_not_empty "target system load" "${an_target_load}" || return $?
          
      213. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    213. 
            

            

                
          
      214. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    214. 
            

            

                # Check if niceload is available
          
      215. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    215. 
            

            

                if ! command -v niceload >/dev/null 2>&1; then
          
      216. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    216. 
            

            

                    echo "${STAMP} ${PARALLEL_PID} ${PARALLEL_JOBSLOT} ${PARALLEL_SEQ}: niceload command not found" >&2
          
      217. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    217. 
            

            

                    return $MISSING_CMD
          
      218. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    218. 
            

            

                fi
          
      219. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    219. 
            

            

                
          
      220. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    220. 
            

            

                # Ensure workers file directory exists
          
      221. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    221. 
            

            

                local workers_dir
          
      222. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    222. 
            

            

                workers_dir=$(dirname "$an_workers")
          
      223. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    223. 
            

            

                if [[ ! -d "$workers_dir" ]]; then
          
      224. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    224. 
            

            

                    mkdir -p "$workers_dir" || return $?
          
      225. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    225. 
            

            

                fi
          
      226. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    226. 
            

            

                
          
      227. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    227. 
            

            

                # Apply niceload to main process if not already controlled
          
      228. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    228. 
            

            

                if ! grep -qs "^${an_mainid} " "${an_workers}" 2>/dev/null; then
          
      229. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    229. 
            

            

                    echo "${an_mainid} main job" >> "${an_workers}"
          
      230. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    230. 
            

            

                    niceload -v --load "${an_target_load}" ${OPT_NICELOAD:-} -p "${an_mainid}" &
          
      231. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    231. 
            

            

                    parallel_log_setting "main process under load control" "${an_mainid}"
          
      232. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    232. 
            

            

                fi
          
      233. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    233. 
            

            

                
          
      234. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    234. 
            

            

                # Apply niceload to all child processes
          
      235. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    235. 
            

            

                local an_kid
          
      236. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    236. 
            

            

                for an_kid in $(kids "${an_mainid}"); do
          
      237. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    237. 
            

            

                    if ! grep -qs "^${an_kid} " "${an_workers}" 2>/dev/null; then
          
      238. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    238. 
            

            

                        echo "${an_kid} child job" >> "${an_workers}"
          
      239. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    239. 
            

            

                        niceload -v --load "${an_target_load}" ${OPT_NICELOAD:-} -p "${an_kid}" &
          
      240. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    240. 
            

            

                        parallel_log_setting "a process under load control" "${an_kid}"
          
      241. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    241. 
            

            

                    fi
          
      242. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    242. 
            

            

                done
          
      243. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    243. 
            

            

                return 0
          
      244. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    244. 
            

            

            }
          
      245. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    245. 
            

            

            export -f apply_niceload

            # return_codes.sh: Standardized exit codes for bump utility scripts

            # This file defines consistent exit codes used across all bump utilities.

            # Exit codes are grouped by category to help identify the type of failure.

            # Exit Code Ranges:
          
      11. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    11. 
            

            

            #   60-69: Missing resources (files, commands, etc.)
          
      12. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    12. 
            

            

            #   70-79: Configuration and safety issues
          
      13. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    13. 
            

            

            #   80-89: System and infrastructure failures
          
      14. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    14. 
            

            

            #   110-119: Signal handling
          
      15. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    15. 
            

            

            
          
      16. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    16. 
            

            

            # Missing Resources (60-69)
          
      17. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    17. 
            

            

            # These codes indicate that a required resource could not be found
          
      18. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    18. 
            
              1
            

            

            MISSING_INPUT=60      # Required input parameter was not provided
          
      19.

              1
            

            

            MISSING_FILE=61       # Required file does not exist

            MISSING_FOLDER=62     # Required directory does not exist

              1
            

            

            MISSING_DISK=63       # Required disk or device not available
          
      22.

              1
            

            

            MISSING_MOUNT=64      # Required mount point not mounted
          
      23. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    23. 
            
              1
            

            

            MISSING_CMD=65        # Required command not found in PATH

    24.

    25.

            function cleanup {
          
      166. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    166. 
            

            

                local c_rc="${1:-0}"
          
      167. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    167. 
            

            

                print_error_rule
          
      168. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    168. 
            

            

                echo "${STAMP}: exiting cleanly with code ${c_rc}. . ." >&2
          
      169. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    169. 
            

            

                
          
      170. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    170. 
            

            

                local cleanfn
          
      171. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    171. 
            

            

                for cleanfn in "${cleanup_functions[@]}"; do
          
      172. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    172. 
            

            

                    if [[ "$cleanfn" == cleanup_* ]]; then
          
      173. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    173. 
            

            

                        if declare -f "$cleanfn" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
          
      174. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    174. 
            

            

                            "$cleanfn" "${c_rc}" || true
          
      175. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    175. 
            

            

                        else
          
      176. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    176. 
            

            

                            echo "${STAMP}: cleanup function $cleanfn not found" >&2
          
      177. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    177. 
            

            

                        fi
          
      178. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    178. 
            

            

                    else
          
      179. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    179. 
            

            

                        echo "${STAMP}: not calling $cleanfn (invalid name)" >&2
          
      180. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    180. 
            

            

                    fi
          
      181. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    181. 
            

            

                done
          
      182. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    182. 
            

            

                echo "${STAMP}: . . . all done with code ${c_rc}" >&2
          
      183. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    183. 
            

            

                # Don't exit during test
          
      184. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    184. 
            

            

            }
          
      185. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    185. 
            

            

            
          
      186. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    186. 
            

            

            # Call cleanup
          
      187. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    187. 
            

            

            cleanup 42 2>/dev/null
          
      188. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    188.

            # Create a subprocess tree to test kids functionality

            if [[ -d "/proc/$$" ]]; then
          
      271.

                # Only test on systems with /proc

                (

    273. 
            

            

                    sleep 10 &

      338.

            function cleanup_recursive_test {
          
      387.

            # Define cleanup that tracks re-entrance

            function cleanup {
          
      399. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    399. 
            

            

                local c_rc="${1:-0}"
          
      400. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    400. 
            

            

                if [[ -n "${CLEANUP_RUNNING:-}" ]]; then
          
      401. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    401. 
            

            

                    echo "GUARD: cleanup already running, preventing recursion" >&2
          
      402. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    402. 
            

            

                    return 1
          
      403. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    403. 
            

            

                fi
          
      404. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    404. 
            

            

                export CLEANUP_RUNNING=1
          
      405. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    405. 
            

            

            
          
      406. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    406. 
            

            

                local cleanfn
          
      407. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    407. 
            

            

                for cleanfn in "${cleanup_functions[@]}"; do
          
      408. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    408. 
            

            

                    if [[ "$cleanfn" == cleanup_* ]]; then
          
      409. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    409. 
            

            

                        if declare -f "$cleanfn" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
          
      410. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    410. 
            

            

                            "$cleanfn" "${c_rc}" 2>&1 || true
          
      411. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    411. 
            

            

                        fi
          
      412. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    412. 
            

            

                    fi
          
      413. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    413. 
            

            

                done
          
      414. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    414. 
            

            

                unset CLEANUP_RUNNING
          
      415. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    415. 
            

            

                return "$c_rc"
          
      416. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    416. 
            

            

            }
          
      417. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    417. 
            

            

            
          
      418. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    418. 
            

            

            # Try to trigger the issue
          
      419. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    419. 
            

            

            cleanup 0 2>/dev/null
          
      420. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    420.

            # Check how many times recursion happened

            else

            fi
          
      427. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    427. 
            

            

            
          
      428. 
        
      
      
        
      
          
    428. 
            

            

            # Restore cleanup
          
      429.
